[Read from /workspace/app-d6jn0ph0piwx/src/hooks/useApkUpdate.ts is successful.]
Here's the result of running `cat -n` on the file: /workspace/app-d6jn0ph0piwx/src/hooks/useApkUpdate.ts:
     1 import { useEffect, useState, useCallback } from 'react';
     2 import { Platform, Linking } from 'react-native';
     3 import { supabase } from '@/client/supabase';
     4 
     5 // 当前 App 数字版本号（与 app.json 的 version 同步维护）
     6 // 每次发新 APK 时，这里也要同步更新
     7 export const CURRENT_VERSION_CODE = 1328;
     8 
     9 export interface ApkVersionInfo {
    10   version_name: string;
    11   version_code: number;
    12   apk_url: string;
    13   release_notes: string;
    14   is_force: boolean;
    15 }
    16 
    17 export type ApkUpdateState =
    18   | { status: 'idle' }
    19   | { status: 'available'; info: ApkVersionInfo; onDownload: () => void; onDismiss: () => void };
    20 
    21 /**
    22  * APK 版本检测 Hook（仅 Android，iOS 不适用）
    23  *
    24  * 从 app_versions 表读取最新版本号，与当前 CURRENT_VERSION_CODE 比较，
    25  * 若服务端版本更高，则返回下载信息和操作回调。
    26  * 点击下载后通过浏览器打开 APK 链接（系统接管下载 + 安装），无需 reloadAsync。
    27  */
    28 export function useApkUpdate(): ApkUpdateState {
    29   const [state, setState] = useState<ApkUpdateState>({ status: 'idle' });
    30 
    31   const check = useCallback(async () => {
    32     // 仅 Android 支持侧载 APK；iOS 不走此逻辑
    33     if (Platform.OS !== 'android') return;
    34 
    35     try {
    36       const { data, error } = await supabase
    37         .from('app_versions')
    38         .select('version_name, version_code, apk_url, release_notes, is_force')
    39         .order('version_code', { ascending: false })
    40         .limit(1)
    41         .maybeSingle();
    42 
    43       if (error || !data) return;
    44       if (data.version_code <= CURRENT_VERSION_CODE) return;
    45 
    46       const info = data as ApkVersionInfo;
    47       setState({
    48         status: 'available',
    49         info,
    50         onDownload: () => {
    51           // 用系统浏览器打开 APK 链接，由系统负责下载和安装引导
    52           // 无需 REQUEST_INSTALL_PACKAGES 权限，浏览器下载后系统自动提示安装
    53           Linking.openURL(info.apk_url);
    54         },
    55         onDismiss: () => {
    56           if (!info.is_force) setState({ status: 'idle' });
    57         },
    58       });
    59     } catch {
    60       // 网络异常静默处理，不影响正常使用
    61     }
    62   }, []);
    63 
    64   useEffect(() => {
    65     // 启动时延迟 3 秒检测，确保网络就绪
    66     const t = setTimeout(check, 3000);
    67     return () => clearTimeout(t);
    68   }, [check]);
    69 
    70   return state;
    71 }
    72 


</observation>

   179 function RootLayoutNav() {
   180   const { session, isLoading } = useSession();
   181   const router = useRouter();
   182 
   183   // 兜底导航：当 session 已从 localStorage 恢复但 sign-in 页未挂载时
   184   // (auth) 路由被 Stack.Protected 封锁，sign-in 的 useEffect 不会触发，
   185   // 需在此处主动跳转，否则用户看到空白屏无法进入应用
   186   useEffect(() => {
   187     if (!isLoading && session) {
   188       router.replace('/(app)/home' as never);
   189     }
   190   // eslint-disable-next-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps
   191   }, [isLoading, session]);
   192 
   193   if (isLoading) {
   194     return <LoadingScreen />;
   195   }
   196 
   197   return (
   198     <Stack screenOptions={{ headerShown: false }}>
   199       {/* 未登录：只显示登录页（未登录时 guard=true 放行） */}
   200       <Stack.Protected guard={!session}>
   201         <Stack.Screen name="index" />
   202         <Stack.Screen name="(auth)" />
   203       </Stack.Protected>
   204       {/* 已登录：进入主业务（已登录时 guard=true 放行） */}
   205       <Stack.Protected guard={!!session}>
   206         <Stack.Screen name="(app)" />
   207       </Stack.Protected>
   208     </Stack>
   209   );
   210 }
   211 
   212 export default function RootLayout() {
   213   return (
   214     <GestureHandlerRootView style={{ flex: 1 }}>
   215       <SessionProvider>
   216         <StatusBar style="light" backgroundColor="#0D1B4B" />
   217         <RootLayoutNav />
   218         <UpdateBanner />
   219         <ApkUpdateBanner />
   220         <PortalHost />

Here's the result of running `cat -n` on the file: /workspace/app-d6jn0ph0piwx/src/hooks/useApkUpdate.ts:
     1 import { useEffect, useState, useCallback } from 'react';
     2 import { Platform, Linking } from 'react-native';
     3 import { supabase } from '@/client/supabase';
     4 
     5 // 当前 App 数字版本号（与 app.json 的 version 同步维护）
     6 // 每次发新 APK 时，这里也要同步更新
     7 export const CURRENT_VERSION_CODE = 1335;
     8 
     9 export interface ApkVersionInfo {
    10   version_name: string;
    11   version_code: number;
    12   apk_url: string;
    13   release_notes: string;
    14   is_force: boolean;
    15 }
    16 
    17 export type ApkUpdateState =
    18   | { status: 'idle' }
    19   | { status: 'available'; info: ApkVersionInfo; onDownload: () => void; onDismiss: () => void };
    20 
    21 /**
    22  * APK 版本检测 Hook（仅 Android，iOS 不适用）
    23  *
    24  * 从 app_versions 表读取最新版本号，与当前 CURRENT_VERSION_CODE 比较，
    25  * 若服务端版本更高，则返回下载信息和操作回调。
    26  * 点击下载后通过浏览器打开 APK 链接（系统接管下载 + 安装），无需 reloadAsync。
    27  * @param skipCheck 传 true 时跳过版本检测（如管理员账号）
    28  */
    29 export function useApkUpdate(skipCheck = false): ApkUpdateState {
    30   const [state, setState] = useState<ApkUpdateState>({ status: 'idle' });
    31 
    32   const check = useCallback(async () => {
    33     // 管理员账号 / iOS 均不检测 APK 更新
    34     if (skipCheck || Platform.OS !== 'android') return;
    35 
    36     try {
    37       const { data, error } = await supabase
    38         .from('app_versions')
    39         .select('version_name, version_code, apk_url, release_notes, is_force')
    40         .order('version_code', { ascending: false })
    41         .limit(1)
    42         .maybeSingle();
    43 
    44       if (error || !data) return;
    45       if (data.version_code <= CURRENT_VERSION_CODE) return;
    46 
    47       const info = data as ApkVersionInfo;
    48       setState({
    49         status: 'available',
    50         info,
    51         onDownload: () => {
    52           // 用系统浏览器打开 APK 链接，由系统负责下载和安装引导
    53           // 无需 REQUEST_INSTALL_PACKAGES 权限，浏览器下载后系统自动提示安装
    54           Linking.openURL(info.apk_url);
    55         },
    56         onDismiss: () => {
    57           if (!info.is_force) setState({ status: 'idle' });
    58         },
    59       });
    60     } catch {
    61       // 网络异常静默处理，不影响正常使用
    62     }
    63   }, [skipCheck]);
    64 
    65   useEffect(() => {
    66     // 启动时延迟 3 秒检测，确保网络就绪
    67     const t = setTimeout(check, 3000);
    68     return () => clearTimeout(t);
    69   }, [check]);
    70 
    71   return state;
    72 }
    73 


</observation>

import { useEffect, useState, useCallback } from 'react';
import { Platform, Linking } from 'react-native';
import Constants from 'expo-constants';
import { supabase } from '@/client/supabase';

/**
 * 从 app.json version 字段（格式 "主.次.构建号"，如 "1.0.1337"）自动解析构建号。
 * EAS 每次打包时 app.json version 已同步更新，无需再手动改此文件。
 */
function parseVersionCode(version?: string | null): number {
  if (!version) return 0;
  const last = version.split('.').pop();
  const code = parseInt(last ?? '', 10);
  return isNaN(code) ? 0 : code;
}

export const CURRENT_VERSION_CODE = parseVersionCode(
  Constants.expoConfig?.version ?? Constants.manifest?.version
);
```

--BEGIN AGENT OBSERVATION--
     1	import { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
     2	import * as Updates from 'expo-updates';
     3	
     4	export type UpdateState =
     5	  | { status: 'idle' }
     6	  | { status: 'ready' }          // 更新已就绪，下次启动生效
     7	  | { status: 'error'; message: string };
     8	
     9	/**
    10	 * 使用 Updates.useUpdates() 监听 OTA 更新状态。
    11	 *
    12	 * 策略：**不调用 reloadAsync()**。
    13	 * reloadAsync() 会触发 Android JS 运行时热重载，导致网络层未就绪，
    14	 * 所有 Supabase 请求连接失败，表现为更新后什么都读取不到。
    15	 *
    16	 * 改为"下次冷启动生效"：更新包在后台静默下载完毕后，
    17	 * 只提示用户"关闭重开即可"，不强制热重载，彻底避免网络断连问题。
    18	 */
    19	export function useAppUpdate(): UpdateState {
    20	  const [dismissed, setDismissed] = useState(false);
    21	
    22	  // 沙盘 / Web / 开发模式 —— 直接返回 idle，不执行任何更新逻辑
    23	  const enabled = !__DEV__ && process.env.EXPO_OS !== 'web' && Updates.isEnabled;
    24	
    25	  const { isUpdatePending } = Updates.useUpdates();
    26	
    27	  // 不再调用 reloadAsync()，更新包已就绪即可，下次冷启动自动加载
    28	  // 无需 useEffect 触发重载
    29	
    30	  if (!enabled || dismissed) return { status: 'idle' };
    31	
    32	  // isUpdatePending=true：包已下载完毕，等待下次启动
    33	  if (isUpdatePending) {
    34	    return { status: 'ready' };
    35	  }
    36	
    37	  return { status: 'idle' };
    38	}

AgentFinishAction(final_thought='# 当前推送更新机制\n\n现在系统里并存**两套**完全不同的更新机制：\n\n---\n\n## ① OTA 热更新（JS Bundle）— `useAppUpdate`\n\n| 项目 | 说明 |\n|---|---|\n| 技术 | `expo-updates` + EAS Update |\n| 触发方式 | App 后台静默下载，EAS 自动推送 |\n| 生效时机 | **不立即热重载**，等用户下次冷启动自动生效 |\n| 适用范围 | **仅 JS/界面变更**，不含原生代码、新权限、新插件 |\n| 特殊逻辑 | 特意禁用了 `reloadAsync()`，原因是热重载后 Supabase 网络层会断连 |\n| 用户感知 | 基本无感，下次打开 App 即加载新代码 |\n\n---\n\n## ② 当前推送更新机制\n\n现在系统里并存**两套**完全不同的更新机制：\n\n---\n\n## ① OTA 热更新（JS Bundle）— `useAppUpdate`\n\n| 项目 | 说明 |\n|---|---|\n| 技术 | `expo-updates` + EAS Update |\n| 触发方式 | App 后台静默下载，EAS 自动推送 |\n| 生效时机 | ──→ OTA 更新（静默推送，冷启动生效）\n```\n\n**目前没有强制更新逻辑**，`is_force` 字段虽然数据库里有，但代码里只是阻止用户关闭弹框，并不会锁死 App。\n\n---\n\n有需要我可以帮你加：强制更新拦截页、OTA 更新进度显示、或者 OTA    400       opacity.value = withTiming(1, { duration: 300, easing: Easing.out(Easing.cubic) });
   401     }, 160);
   402   }, [value, opacity]);
   403 
   404   const animStyle = useAnimatedStyle(() => ({ opacity: opacity.value }));
   405 
   406   return (
   407     <Animated.Text style={[{ color: '#fff', fontSize: 20, fontWeight: '700', lineHeight: 24 }, style, animStyle]}>
   408       {display}
   409     </Animated.Text>
   410   );
   411 }

   413 // 统计卡片脉冲光点
   414 function PulseDot({ color }: { color: string }) {
   415   const scale = useSharedValue(1);
   416   const opacity = useSharedValue(0.9);
   417   useEffect(() => {
   418     scale.value = withRepeat(withSequence(
   419       withTiming(1.5, { duration: 900, easing: Easing.out(Easing.ease) }),
   420       withTiming(1, { duration: 900, easing: Easing.in(Easing.ease) }),
   421     ), -1, false);
   422     opacity.value = withRepeat(withSequence(
   423       withTiming(0.3, { duration: 900 }),
   424       withTiming(0.9, { duration: 900 }),
   425     ), -1, false);
   426   }, [scale, opacity]);
   427   const dotStyle = useAnimatedStyle(() => ({
   428     transform: [{ scale: scale.value }],
   429     opacity: opacity.value,
   430   }));
   431   return (
   432     <Animated.View style={[{ width: 8, height: 8, borderRadius: 4, backgroundColor: color }, dotStyle]} />
   433   );
   434 }

   181 function RootLayoutNav() {
   182   const { session, isLoading } = useSession();
   183   const router = useRouter();
   184 
   185   // 兜底导航：当 session 已从 localStorage 恢复但 sign-in 页未挂载时
   186   // (auth) 路由被 Stack.Protected 封锁，sign-in 的 useEffect 不会触发，
   187   // 需在此处主动跳转，否则用户看到空白屏无法进入应用
   188   useEffect(() => {
   189     if (!isLoading && session) {
   190       router.replace('/(app)/home' as never);
   191     }
   192   // eslint-disable-next-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps
   193   }, [isLoading, session]);
   194 
   195   if (isLoading) {
   196     return <LoadingScreen />;
   197   }
   198 
   199   return (
   200     <Stack screenOptions={{ headerShown: false }}>
   201       {/* 未登录：只显示登录页（未登录时 guard=true 放行） */}
   202       <Stack.Protected guard={!session}>
   203         <Stack.Screen name="index" />
   204         <Stack.Screen name="(auth)" />
   205       </Stack.Protected>
   206       {/* 已登录：进入主业务（已登录时 guard=true 放行） */}
   207       <Stack.Protected guard={!!session}>
   208         <Stack.Screen name="(app)" />
   209       </Stack.Protected>
   210     </Stack>
   211   );
   212 }
   213 
   214 export default function RootLayout() {
   215   return (
   216     <GestureHandlerRootView style={{ flex: 1 }}>
   217       <SessionProvider>
   218         <StatusBar style="light" backgroundColor="#0D1B4B" />
   219         <RootLayoutNav />
   220         <UpdateBanner />
   221         <ApkUpdateBanner />
   222         <PortalHost />
   223       </SessionProvider>
   224     </GestureHandlerRootView>
   225   );
   226 }

[Read from /workspace/app-d6jn0ph0piwx/src/hooks/useApkUpdate.ts is successful.]
Here's the result of running `cat -n` on the file: /workspace/app-d6jn0ph0piwx/src/hooks/useApkUpdate.ts:
     1 import { useEffect, useState, useCallback } from 'react';
     2 import { Platform, Linking } from 'react-native';
     3 import Constants from 'expo-constants';
     4 import { supabase } from '@/client/supabase';
     5 
     6 /**
     7  * 从 app.json version 字段（格式 "主.次.构建号"，如 "1.0.1337"）自动解析构建号。
     8  * EAS 每次打包时 app.json version 已同步更新，无需再手动改此文件。
     9  */
    10 function parseVersionCode(version?: string | null): number {
    11   if (!version) return 0;
    12   const last = version.split('.').pop();
    13   const code = parseInt(last ?? '', 10);
    14   return isNaN(code) ? 0 : code;
    15 }
    16 
    17 export const CURRENT_VERSION_CODE = parseVersionCode(
    18   Constants.expoConfig?.version
    19 );
    20 
    21 export interface ApkVersionInfo {
    22   version_name: string;
    23   version_code: number;
    24   apk_url: string;
    25   release_notes: string;
    26   is_force: boolean;
    27 }
    28 
    29 export type ApkUpdateState =
    30   | { status: 'idle' }
    31   | { status: 'available'; info: ApkVersionInfo; onDownload: () => void; onDismiss: () => void };
    32 
    33 /**
    34  * APK 版本检测 Hook（仅 Android，iOS 不适用）
    35  *
    36  * 从 app_versions 表读取最新版本号，与当前 CURRENT_VERSION_CODE 比较，
    37  * 若服务端版本更高，则返回下载信息和操作回调。
    38  * 点击下载后通过浏览器打开 APK 链接（系统接管下载 + 安装），无需 reloadAsync。
    39  * @param skipCheck 传 true 时跳过版本检测（如管理员账号）
    40  */
    41 export function useApkUpdate(skipCheck = false): ApkUpdateState {
    42   const [state, setState] = useState<ApkUpdateState>({ status: 'idle' });
    43 
    44   const check = useCallback(async () => {
    45     // 管理员账号 / iOS 均不检测 APK 更新
    46     if (skipCheck || Platform.OS !== 'android') return;
    47 
    48     try {
    49       const { data, error } = await supabase
    50         .from('app_versions')
    51         .select('version_name, version_code, apk_url, release_notes, is_force')
    52         .order('version_code', { ascending: false })
    53         .limit(1)
    54         .maybeSingle();
    55 
    56       if (error || !data) return;
    57       if (data.version_code <= CURRENT_VERSION_CODE) return;
    58 
    59       const info = data as ApkVersionInfo;
    60       setState({
    61         status: 'available',
    62         info,
    63         onDownload: () => {
    64           // 用系统浏览器打开 APK 链接，由系统负责下载和安装引导
    65           // 无需 REQUEST_INSTALL_PACKAGES 权限，浏览器下载后系统自动提示安装
    66           Linking.openURL(info.apk_url);
    67         },
    68         onDismiss: () => {
    69           if (!info.is_force) setState({ status: 'idle' });
    70         },
    71       });
    72     } catch {
    73       // 网络异常静默处理，不影响正常使用
    74     }
    75   }, [skipCheck]);
    76 
    77   useEffect(() => {
    78     // 启动时延迟 3 秒检测，确保网络就绪
    79     const t = setTimeout(check, 3000);
    80     return () => clearTimeout(t);
    81   }, [check]);
    82 
    83   return state;
    84 }
    85

// ── APK 版本更新弹窗（Android 专属，管理员跳过）──────────────────
function ApkUpdateBanner() {
  const { isAdmin, isPermanentAdmin } = useSession();
  // 管理员账号（含临时管理员）发布版本，自己无需收到更新提示
  const update = useApkUpdate(isAdmin || isPermanentAdmin);
  if (update.status !== 'available') return null;
  const { info, onDownload, onDismiss } = update;

  return (
    <Modal transparent animationType="fade" visible>
      <View style={{
        flex: 1, backgroundColor: 'rgba(0,0,0,0.6)',

New String: ```
// ── APK 版本更新弹窗（Android 专属，管理员跳过）──────────────────
// 应用内下载 + 进度条，不借助浏览器
function ApkUpdateBanner() {
  const { isAdmin, isPermanentAdmin } = useSession();
  const update = useApkUpdate(isAdmin || isPermanentAdmin);

  type DlPhase = 'info' | 'downloading' | 'done' | 'error';
  const [phase, setPhase]               = useState<DlPhase>('info');
  const [progress, setProgress]         = useState(0);           // 0-1
  const [bytesWritten, setBytesWritten] = useState(0);
  const [bytesTotal, setBytesTotal]     = useState(0);
  const [speedBps, setSpeedBps]         = useState(0);
  const [errorMsg, setErrorMsg]         = useState('');
  const [barWidth, setBarWidth]         = useState(0);           // 进度条容器实测宽度

  const downloadRef  = useRef<FileSystem.DownloadResumable | null>(null);
  const lastBytesRef = useRef(0);
  const lastTimeRef  = useRef(Date.now());

  // ── 进度条闪光动画 ──
  const shimmerOffset = useSharedValue(0);
  const shimmerStyle  = useAnimatedStyle(() => ({
    transform: [
      { translateX: interpolate(shimmerOffset.value, [0, 1], [-70, barWidth + 70]) },
    ],
    opacity: 0.55,
  }));
  useEffect(() => {
    if (phase === 'downloading') {
      shimmerOffset.value = withRepeat(
        withTiming(1, { duration: 1300, easing: Easing.linear }),
        -1, false,
      );
    } else {
      shimmerOffset.value = 0;
    }
  }, [phase, shimmerOffset]);

  // ── 工具函数 ──
  const fmtBytes = (b: number) =>
    b < 1024 * 1024 ? `${(b / 1024).toFixed(1)} KB` : `${(b / (1024 * 1024)).toFixed(1)} MB`;
  const fmtSpeed = (bps: number) =>
    bps < 1024 * 1024 ? `${(bps / 1024).toFixed(0)} KB/s` : `${(bps / (1024 * 1024)).toFixed(1)} MB/s`;
  const etaSec = speedBps > 0 && bytesTotal > bytesWritten
    ? Math.ceil((bytesTotal - bytesWritten) / speedBps) : null;
  const etaStr = etaSec === null ? '' : etaSec > 60 ? `约 ${Math.ceil(etaSec / 60)} 分钟` : `约 ${etaSec} 秒`;

  // ── 下载逻辑 ──
  const startDownload = async () => {
    if (process.env.EXPO_OS !== 'android') return;
    setPhase('downloading');
    setProgress(0);
    setSpeedBps(0);
    lastBytesRef.current = 0;
    lastTimeRef.current  = Date.now();

    if (!update.info) return;
    const destPath = (FileSystem.cacheDirectory ?? '') + 'vehicle_update.apk';

    const dl = FileSystem.createDownloadResumable(
      update.info.apk_url,
      destPath,
      {},
      ({ totalBytesWritten: w, totalBytesExpectedToWrite: t }) => {
        setProgress(t > 0 ? w / t : 0);
        setBytesWritten(w);
        setBytesTotal(t);
        const now = Date.now();
        const elapsed = (now - lastTimeRef.current) / 1000;
        if (elapsed >= 0.4) {
          setSpeedBps((w - lastBytesRef.current) / elapsed);
          lastBytesRef.current = w;
          lastTimeRef.current  = now;
        }
      },
    );
    downloadRef.current = dl;

    try {
      const result = await dl.downloadAsync();
      if (result?.uri) {
        setPhase('done');
        setProgress(1);
        // 短暂展示完成态后唤起系统安装器
        setTimeout(async () => {
          try {
            const { startActivityAsync } = await import('expo-intent-launcher');
            const contentUri = await FileSystem.getContentUriAsync(result.uri);
            await startActivityAsync('android.intent.action.VIEW', {
              data: contentUri,
              flags: 1,
              type: 'application/vnd.android.package-archive',
            });
          } catch {
            // 安装器关闭或取消 → 恢复初始态
          } finally {
            setPhase('info');
          }
        }, 900);
      } else {
        setPhase('error');
        setErrorMsg('下载失败，请稍后重试');
      }
    } catch (e: unknown) {
      const msg = e instanceof Error ? e.message : '';
      if (msg.toLowerCase().includes('cancel') || msg.toLowerCase().includes('abort')) {
        setPhase('info');
      } else {
        setPhase('error');
        setErrorMsg('下载出错，请检查网络连接后重试');
      }
    }
  };

  const cancelDownload = async () => {
    try { await downloadRef.current?.cancelAsync(); } catch { /* ignore */ }
    downloadRef.current = null;
    setPhase('info');
    setProgress(0);
  };

  if (update.status !== 'available') return null;
  const { info, onDismiss } = update;
  const pct = Math.round(progress * 100);

  return (
    <Modal transparent animationType="fade" visible>
      <View style={{
        flex: 1, backgroundColor: 'rgba(0,0,0,0.72)',
        alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'center', paddingHorizontal: 22,
      }}>
        {/* ── 卡片容器 ── */}
        <View style={{
          width: '100%', borderRadius: 22, overflow: 'hidden',
          borderWidth: 1,
          borderColor: phase === 'done'
            ? 'rgba(34,197,94,0.50)'
            : phase === 'error'
            ? 'rgba(239,68,68,0.45)'
            : phase === 'downloading'
            ? 'rgba(59,130,246,0.55)'
            : 'rgba(59,130,246,0.38)',
        }}>
          <LinearGradient
            colors={['#0F1E3A', '#131B30', '#0D1525']}
            start={{ x: 0, y: 0 }} end={{ x: 1, y: 1 }}
            style={{ padding: 22, gap: 18 }}
          >

            {/* ════════════════ 阶段：info ════════════════ */}
            {phase === 'info' && (<>
              {/* 标题行 */}
              <View style={{ flexDirection: 'row', alignItems: 'center', gap: 13 }}>
                <View style={{
                  width: 48, height: 48, borderRadius: 14,
                  backgroundColor: 'rgba(37,99,235,0.20)',
                  alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'center',
                  borderWidth: 1, borderColor: 'rgba(96,165,250,0.30)',
                }}>
                  <Text style={{ fontSize: 24 }}>📦</Text>
                </View>
                <View style={{ flex: 1 }}>
                  <Text style={{ color: '#F1F5F9', fontSize: 16, fontWeight: '800', letterSpacing: 0.2 }}>
                    发现新版本 v{info.version_name}
                  </Text>
                  <Text style={{ color: '#475569', fontSize: 12, marginTop: 3 }}>
                    车辆信息系统
                  </Text>
                </View>
                {info.is_force && (
                  <View style={{
                    backgroundColor: 'rgba(239,68,68,0.18)', borderRadius: 8,
                    paddingHorizontal: 8, paddingVertical: 4,
                    borderWidth: 1, borderColor: 'rgba(239,68,68,0.38)',
                  }}>
                    <Text style={{ color: '#F87171', fontSize: 11, fontWeight: '800' }}>强制更新</Text>
                  </View>
                )}
              </View>

              {/* 更新内容 */}
              <View style={{
                backgroundColor: 'rgba(255,255,255,0.04)', borderRadius: 12,
                padding: 14, borderWidth: 1, borderColor: 'rgba(255,255,255,0.07)',
              }}>
                <Text style={{ color: '#64748B', fontSize: 11, marginBottom: 7, fontWeight: '700', letterSpacing: 0.8, textTransform: 'uppercase' }}>
                  更新内容
                </Text>
                <Text style={{ color: '#CBD5E1', fontSize: 13, lineHeight: 21 }}>
                  {info.release_notes}
                </Text>
              </View>

              <Text style={{ color: '#334155', fontSize: 11, textAlign: 'center', letterSpacing: 0.3 }}>
                将在应用内直接下载，无需跳转浏览器
              </Text>

              {/* 操作按钮 */}
              <View style={{ flexDirection: 'row', gap: 10 }}>
                {!info.is_force && (
                  <Pressable
                    onPress={onDismiss}
                    style={{
                      flex: 1, backgroundColor: 'rgba(255,255,255,0.06)',
                      borderRadius: 12, paddingVertical: 14, alignItems: 'center',
                      borderWidth: 1, borderColor: 'rgba(255,255,255,0.10)',
                    }}
                  >
                    <Text style={{ color: '#64748B', fontSize: 14, fontWeight: '600' }}>稍后更新</Text>
                  </Pressable>
                )}
                <Pressable
                  onPress={startDownload}
                  style={{ flex: 2, borderRadius: 12, overflow: 'hidden' }}
                >
                  <LinearGradient
                    colors={['#1D4ED8', '#2563EB', '#3B82F6']}
                    start={{ x: 0, y: 0 }} end={{ x: 1, y: 0 }}
                    style={{
                      paddingVertical: 14,
                      flexDirection: 'row', alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'center', gap: 8,
                    }}
                  >
                    <Text style={{ fontSize: 16 }}>⬇️</Text>
                    <Text style={{ color: '#fff', fontSize: 14, fontWeight: '800', letterSpacing: 0.3 }}>
                      立即下载安装
                    </Text>
                  </LinearGradient>
                </Pressable>
              </View>
            </>)}

            {/* ════════════════ 阶段：downloading ════════════════ */}
            {phase === 'downloading' && (<>
              {/* 顶部信息 */}
              <View style={{ flexDirection: 'row', alignItems: 'center', gap: 12 }}>
                <View style={{
                  width: 44, height: 44, borderRadius: 12,
                  backgroundColor: 'rgba(37,99,235,0.18)',
                  alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'center',
                  borderWidth: 1, borderColor: 'rgba(96,165,250,0.28)',
                }}>
                  <ActivityIndicator size="small" color="#60A5FA" />
                </View>
                <View style={{ flex: 1 }}>
                  <Text style={{ color: '#F1F5F9', fontSize: 15, fontWeight: '800' }}>
                    正在下载  v{info.version_name}
                  </Text>
                  <Text style={{ color: '#475569', fontSize: 12, marginTop: 2 }}>
                    车辆信息系统
                  </Text>
                </View>
                <Text style={{ color: '#60A5FA', fontSize: 24, fontWeight: '900', letterSpacing: -1 }}>
                  {pct}%
                </Text>
              </View>

              {/* 进度条 */}
              <View
                onLayout={e => setBarWidth(e.nativeEvent.layout.width)}
                style={{
                  height: 10, backgroundColor: 'rgba(255,255,255,0.07)',
                  borderRadius: 5, overflow: 'hidden',
                }}
              >
                {/* 填充 */}
                <View style={{
                  position: 'absolute', top: 0, bottom: 0, left: 0,
                  width: `${pct}%` as `${number}%`,
                  borderRadius: 5, overflow: 'hidden',
                }}>
                  <LinearGradient
                    colors={['#1D4ED8', '#3B82F6', '#60A5FA']}
                    start={{ x: 0, y: 0 }} end={{ x: 1, y: 0 }}
                    style={{ flex: 1 }}
                  />
                  {/* 闪光条 */}
                  <Animated.View style={[shimmerStyle, {
                    position: 'absolute', top: 0, bottom: 0, width: 55,
                  }]}>
                    <LinearGradient
                      colors={['transparent', 'rgba(255,255,255,0.55)', 'transparent']}
                      start={{ x: 0, y: 0 }} end={{ x: 1, y: 0 }}
                      style={{ flex: 1 }}
                    />
                  </Animated.View>
                </View>
              </View>

              {/* 速度 / 大小 / 预计时间 */}
              <View style={{ gap: 5 }}>
                <View style={{ flexDirection: 'row', alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'space-between' }}>
                  <View style={{ flexDirection: 'row', alignItems: 'center', gap: 6 }}>
                    {/* 速度徽章 */}
                    {speedBps > 0 && (
                      <View style={{
                        flexDirection: 'row', alignItems: 'center', gap: 4,
                        backgroundColor: 'rgba(59,130,246,0.15)', borderRadius: 6,
                        paddingHorizontal: 8, paddingVertical: 3,
                        borderWidth: 1, borderColor: 'rgba(59,130,246,0.30)',
                      }}>
                        <Text style={{ color: '#93C5FD', fontSize: 10, fontWeight: '700' }}>▼</Text>
                        <Text style={{ color: '#93C5FD', fontSize: 12, fontWeight: '800' }}>
                          {fmtSpeed(speedBps)}
                        </Text>
                      </View>
                    )}
                    {/* 字节进度 */}
                    {bytesTotal > 0 && (
                      <Text style={{ color: '#64748B', fontSize: 12 }}>
                        {fmtBytes(bytesWritten)} / {fmtBytes(bytesTotal)}
                      </Text>
                    )}
                  </View>
                  {/* ETA */}
                  {!!etaStr && (
                    <Text style={{ color: '#475569', fontSize: 11 }}>预计 {etaStr}</Text>
                  )}
                </View>
              </View>

              {/* 取消按钮 */}
              <Pressable
                onPress={cancelDownload}
                style={{
                  alignSelf: 'center',
                  backgroundColor: 'rgba(255,255,255,0.06)', borderRadius: 10,
                  paddingHorizontal: 28, paddingVertical: 11,
                  borderWidth: 1, borderColor: 'rgba(255,255,255,0.09)',
                }}
              >
                <Text style={{ color: '#64748B', fontSize: 13, fontWeight: '600' }}>取消下载</Text>
              </Pressable>
            </>)}

            {/* ════════════════ 阶段：done ════════════════ */}
            {phase === 'done' && (
              <View style={{ alignItems: 'center', gap: 14, paddingVertical: 10 }}>
                <View style={{
                  width: 60, height: 60, borderRadius: 30,
                  backgroundColor: 'rgba(34,197,94,0.18)',
                  alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'center',
                  borderWidth: 1.5, borderColor: 'rgba(34,197,94,0.45)',
                }}>
                  <Text style={{ fontSize: 30 }}>✅</Text>
                </View>
                <View style={{ alignItems: 'center', gap: 5 }}>
                  <Text style={{ color: '#F1F5F9', fontSize: 16, fontWeight: '800' }}>下载完成！</Text>
                  <Text style={{ color: '#64748B', fontSize: 13 }}>正在启动安装程序…</Text>
                </View>
                {/* 全条进度（100%）*/}
                <View style={{ width: '100%', height: 6, backgroundColor: 'rgba(255,255,255,0.06)', borderRadius: 3, overflow: 'hidden' }}>
                  <LinearGradient
                    colors={['#16A34A', '#22C55E', '#4ADE80']}
                    start={{ x: 0, y: 0 }} end={{ x: 1, y: 0 }}
                    style={{ width: '100%', height: 6 }}
                  />
                </View>
              </View>
            )}

            {/* ════════════════ 阶段：error ════════════════ */}
            {phase === 'error' && (<>
              <View style={{ alignItems: 'center', gap: 12, paddingTop: 4 }}>
                <Text style={{ fontSize: 36 }}>⚠️</Text>
                <Text style={{ color: '#F87171', fontSize: 15, fontWeight: '700' }}>下载失败</Text>
                <Text style={{ color: '#64748B', fontSize: 13, textAlign: 'center' }}>{errorMsg}</Text>
              </View>
              <View style={{ flexDirection: 'row', gap: 10 }}>
                {!info.is_force && (
                  <Pressable
                    onPress={onDismiss}
                    style={{
                      flex: 1, backgroundColor: 'rgba(255,255,255,0.06)',
                      borderRadius: 12, paddingVertical: 14, alignItems: 'center',
                      borderWidth: 1, borderColor: 'rgba(255,255,255,0.10)',
                    }}
                  >
                    <Text style={{ color: '#64748B', fontSize: 14, fontWeight: '600' }}>取消</Text>
                  </Pressable>
                )}
                <Pressable
                  onPress={startDownload}
                  style={{ flex: 2, borderRadius: 12, overflow: 'hidden' }}
                >
                  <LinearGradient
                    colors={['#B91C1C', '#DC2626', '#EF4444']}
                    start={{ x: 0, y: 0 }} end={{ x: 1, y: 0 }}
                    style={{ paddingVertical: 14, alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'center' }}
                  >
                    <Text style={{ color: '#fff', fontSize: 14, fontWeight: '800' }}>重新下载</Text>
                  </LinearGradient>
                </Pressable>
              </View>
            </>)}

          </LinearGradient>
        </View>
      </View>
    </Modal>
  );
}
```

</action>

export declare class Paths extends PathUtilities {
    /**
     * A property containing the cache directory – a place to store files that can be deleted by the system when the device runs low on storage.
     */
    static get cache(): Directory;
    /**
     * A property containing the bundle directory – the directory where assets bundled with the application are stored.
     */
    static get bundle(): Directory;
    /**
     * A property containing the document directory – a place to store files that are safe from being deleted by the system.
     */
    static get document(): Directory;
    static get appleSharedContainers(): Record<string, Directory>;
    /**
     * A property that represents the total space on device's internal storage, represented in bytes.
     */
    static get totalDiskSpace(): number;
    /**
     * A property that represents the available space on device's internal storage, represented in bytes.
     */
    static get availableDiskSpace(): number;
    /**
     * Returns an object that indicates if the specified path represents a directory.
     */
    static info(...uris: string[]): PathInfo;
}

    95 // 应用内下载 + 进度条，不借助浏览器
    96 function ApkUpdateBanner() {
    97   const { isAdmin, isPermanentAdmin } = useSession();
    98   const update = useApkUpdate(isAdmin || isPermanentAdmin);
    99 
   100   type DlPhase = 'info' | 'downloading' | 'done' | 'error';
   101   const [phase, setPhase]               = useState<DlPhase>('info');
   102   const [progress, setProgress]         = useState(0);           // 0-1
   103   const [bytesWritten, setBytesWritten] = useState(0);
   104   const [bytesTotal, setBytesTotal]     = useState(0);
   105   const [speedBps, setSpeedBps]         = useState(0);
   106   const [errorMsg, setErrorMsg]         = useState('');
   107   const [barWidth, setBarWidth]         = useState(0);           // 进度条容器实测宽度
   108 
   109   // eslint-disable-next-line @typescript-eslint/no-explicit-any
   110   const downloadRef  = useRef<any>(null);
   111   const lastBytesRef = useRef(0);
   112   const lastTimeRef  = useRef(Date.now());
   113 
   114   // ── 进度条闪光动画 ──
   115   const shimmerOffset = useSharedValue(0);
   116   const shimmerStyle  = useAnimatedStyle(() => ({
   117     transform: [
   118       { translateX: interpolate(shimmerOffset.value, [0, 1], [-70, barWidth + 70]) },
   119     ],
   120     opacity: 0.55,
   121   }));
   122   useEffect(() => {
   123     if (phase === 'downloading') {
   124       shimmerOffset.value = withRepeat(
   125         withTiming(1, { duration: 1300, easing: Easing.linear }),
   126         -1, false,
   127       );
   128     } else {
   129       shimmerOffset.value = 0;
   130     }
   131   }, [phase, shimmerOffset]);
   132 
   133   // ── 工具函数 ──
   134   const fmtBytes = (b: number) =>
   135     b < 1024 * 1024 ? `${(b / 1024).toFixed(1)} KB` : `${(b / (1024 * 1024)).toFixed(1)} MB`;
   136   const fmtSpeed = (bps: number) =>
   137     bps < 1024 * 1024 ? `${(bps / 1024).toFixed(0)} KB/s` : `${(bps / (1024 * 1024)).toFixed(1)} MB/s`;
   138   const etaSec = speedBps > 0 && bytesTotal > bytesWritten
   139     ? Math.ceil((bytesTotal - bytesWritten) / speedBps) : null;
   140   const etaStr = etaSec === null ? '' : etaSec > 60 ? `约 ${Math.ceil(etaSec / 60)} 分钟` : `约 ${etaSec} 秒`;
   141 
   142   // ── 下载逻辑 ──
   143   const startDownload = async () => {
   144     if (process.env.EXPO_OS !== 'android') return;
   145     setPhase('downloading');
   146     setProgress(0);
   147     setSpeedBps(0);
   148     lastBytesRef.current = 0;
   149     lastTimeRef.current  = Date.now();
   150 
   151     if (!update.info) return;
   152     const destPath = (FileSystem.cacheDirectory ?? '') + 'vehicle_update.apk';
   153 
   154     const dl = FileSystem.createDownloadResumable(
   155       update.info.apk_url,
   156       destPath,
   157       {},
   158       ({ totalBytesWritten: w, totalBytesExpectedToWrite: t }) => {
   159         setProgress(t > 0 ? w / t : 0);
   160         setBytesWritten(w);
   161         setBytesTotal(t);
   162         const now = Date.now();
   163         const elapsed = (now - lastTimeRef.current) / 1000;
   164         if (elapsed >= 0.4) {
   165           setSpeedBps((w - lastBytesRef.current) / elapsed);
   166           lastBytesRef.current = w;
   167           lastTimeRef.current  = now;
   168         }
   169       },
   170     );
   171     downloadRef.current = dl;
   172 
   173     try {
   174       const result = await dl.downloadAsync();
   175       if (result?.uri) {
   176         setPhase('done');
   177         setProgress(1);
   178         // 短暂展示完成态后唤起系统安装器
   179         setTimeout(async () => {
   180           try {
   181             const { startActivityAsync } = await import('expo-intent-launcher');
   182             const contentUri = await FileSystem.getContentUriAsync(result.uri);
   183             await startActivityAsync('android.intent.action.VIEW', {
   184               data: contentUri,
   185               flags: 1,
   186               type: 'application/vnd.android.package-archive',
   187             });
   188           } catch {
   189             // 安装器关闭或取消 → 恢复初始态
   190           } finally {
   191             setPhase('info');
   192           }
   193         }, 900);
   194       } else {
   195         setPhase('error');
   196         setErrorMsg('下载失败，请稍后重试');
   197       }
   198     } catch (e: unknown) {
   199       const msg = e instanceof Error ? e.message : '';
   200       if (msg.toLowerCase().includes('cancel') || msg.toLowerCase().includes('abort')) {
   201         setPhase('info');
   202       } else {
   203         setPhase('error');
   204         setErrorMsg('下载出错，请检查网络连接后重试');
   205       }
   206     }
   207   };
   208 
   209   const cancelDownload = async () => {
   210     try { await downloadRef.current?.cancelAsync(); } catch { /* ignore */ }
